Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any spectroscopy module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 387. The projective resolution has length at most 492 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on spectroscopy structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 387. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 492. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the spectroscopy structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 851. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 968 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 851 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spectroscopy structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 968. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
